- A set of spatial propositions for analysis

## SPATIAL PROPOSITIONS:
Prop 1: If a person is inside a room and a wall is between the person and a door, then the person cannot pass through the door without crossing the wall.
Prop 2: If an object is on top of another object, then the top object is above the bottom object and the bottom object is below the top object.
Prop 3: If a person is behind a wall and the wall is between the person and a location, then the person is not at the location.
Prop 4: If a person enters a room through a door and the door is locked from the inside, then the person cannot exit the room through the same door without unlocking it.
Prop 5: If a weapon is used to shoot a person and the person is behind an object, then the object is between the weapon and the person.

**Step 4:** If none of the listed propositions apply, explain the spatial reasoning used to identify the contradiction.

------------------------------

You are provided with:
- A list of characters, evidences and testimonies
- A set of spatial propositions for analysis

Prop 1: If a person is in a room and a wall is between the person and an object, then the person cannot see the object.
Prop 2: If a person is behind a door and the door is locked, then the person cannot pass through the door.
Prop 3: If a weapon is used to stab a person and the person is found in a location, then the stabbing occurred at that location.
Prop 4: If a person is in a location and a camera is recording the location, then the person's actions in the location can be seen on the camera.

**Step 4:** The spatial reasoning used here involves understanding the implications of finding a body in a specific location and the testimonies about the events leading up to the crime. It suggests that either the body was moved after the stabbing, or there is an inconsistency in the testimonies or evidences regarding the location of the crime.

**Step 5:** Given the analysis, the contradiction lies between Evidence 14 (Terran's Autopsy Report), which states the victim was found in the third-floor boarding lounge, and Testimony 3 by Ponco, which implies the crime occurred near the round window on the workbench side of the room in the robotics lab. **Step 4:** If none of the listed propositions apply, explain the spatial reasoning used to identify the contradiction.

The evidence suggests a severe and immediate threat to Clay's life due to the ruptured oxygen tank, which contradicts the scenario where Starbuck could support him and evacuate him as if he were merely passed out.

**Step 5:** Provide your answer in JSON format.

**Step 4:** If none of the listed propositions apply, explain the spatial reasoning used to identify the contradiction.

Prop 1: If a person is in a room and a wall is between the person and an object, then the person cannot see the object.
Prop 2: If a person is holding a weapon and is in the same room as another person, then the person holding the weapon is near the other person.
Prop 3: If a door is locked and a person is on the other side of the door, then the person cannot pass through the door.
Prop 4: If an object is inside a location and a person is also inside the same location, then the person is near the object.
Prop 5: If a person is behind another person and a weapon is fired from behind, then the person in front is at risk of being hit by the weapon.

Prop 2: If an object is used as a weapon to hit a person from behind, then the person is in front of the object.
Prop 3: If a person falls into an object after being hit, then the object is at the location where the person fell.
Prop 4: If a person is found in a room with a broken object, and the person has blood on them, then the broken object is likely at the same location as the person.
Prop 5: If a person is holding a weapon in front of a body, and the body is in a location, then the person is at the same location as the body.
